## Slide Design Audit: new-01 through new-18

### Page-by-Page Report

**new-01 (Title)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. The dark-navy frametitle bar is absent (title slide correctly omits it), the blue title box and footer band match the reference. However the footer separator shows "答辩人: 王致尧 | 指导教师: 周纯杰 (HUST)" — the pipe character "|" is used instead of the em-dash or double-space spacing seen in the reference footer. Minor but inconsistent with ref-01.
- Density: clean — no issue.
- Hierarchy: "谢谢！" equivalent is the title; identifiable instantly. Yes.

**new-02 (汇报提纲)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Blue numbered balls and dark-navy frametitle bar present and correct. Only 4 items (vs ref-02's 5) — appropriate tightening. Clean.
- Density: 4 items, well-spaced. No violation.
- Hierarchy: Yes, outline structure clear at a glance.

**new-03 (研究背景)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Two rounded blocks with dark-blue title strip — correct. Bold dark-blue inline emphasis present. However the pipeline diagram ("场景 → 攻击 → 防护 → 闭环结果") sits sandwiched between the two blocks with no visual container, and the box borders on the pipeline nodes appear to use a lighter blue/gray rather than the consistent thin-border white-fill TikZ style described. The diagram floats somewhat unanchored.
- Density: 3 bullets in block 1, plus inline diagram, plus block 2 with multi-line text. Total content is at the edge — borderline 5-element overload.
- Hierarchy: The takeaway ("本文切入点") is in the bottom block, readable but below the fold of the eye-path. Marginally acceptable. Yes.

**new-04 (研究对象) — SPECIAL FOCUS**
- Style match: PARTIAL. The frametitle ("研究对象：定位—控制闭环") is correct, dark-navy bar, one diagram + one block. Split-attention is largely resolved — the diagram sits alone above the block without competing text. Good improvement.
- Issue: large vertical white space between frametitle and diagram (roughly one-third of slide height is blank), making the slide feel under-filled. The diagram nodes ("GNSS/IMU 观测", "攻击注入", "EKF 融合定位", "路径跟踪控制", "越界/碰撞轨迹偏离") are thin-border white-fill boxes — consistent. Sub-labels below each node in gray — good.
- Density: one diagram + one block. No violation. ONE primary visual primitive. Yes.
- Hierarchy: Yes, diagram is the immediate eye-catcher, block caption follows.
- Issue: The empty upper half creates awkward dead space. The diagram could be shifted up or the block enlarged.

**new-05 (现有问题与本文回答的三个问题)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Frametitle bar correct. Blue numbered enumerate inside rounded block — correct. Bold dark-blue inline emphasis on key phrases (最小有效边界, 静态攻击参数, 防护开启后). No orange visible.
- Density: Two inline sub-sections ("已有基础" / "仍待解决") with plain text above the block, then a block with 3 numbered items. This is 2 + 2 + 3 = borderline 7 total content pieces. The sub-headers "已有基础" and "仍待解决" are plain dark-blue text without a visual container — they float as freestanding lines.
- Hierarchy: The main block "本文回答的三个问题" is the single takeaway. Yes, identifiable.
- Issue: "已有基础" and "仍待解决" labels in the middle of the slide are stylistically orphaned — they look like colored plain text with no block wrapper, which creates inconsistency with the rest of the deck where such sections use block environments.

**new-06 (技术路线)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Correct frametitle, correct block. The pipeline diagram uses a two-row flow (1→2→3→4 then 7←6←5). This layout is clean. The block ("阶段说明") has bold inline emphasis. Consistent style.
- Density: one diagram + one block. No violation. Good.
- Hierarchy: Yes. The finding block anchors the takeaway.

**new-07 (实验平台)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Correct frametitle, no block used — instead uses a two-column layout with diagram left, "平台特性" heading and bullets right, and then an italic footnote below. The italic footnote text is a gray-ish tone, appropriately secondary.
- Density: diagram + 4 bullets + footnote. Acceptable. However the "平台特性" sub-header is plain text (dark blue, no block wrapper), inconsistent with pages 5, 9 etc. which sometimes use colored label text.
- Hierarchy: Yes.
- Issue: the right-column bullets wrap to two lines each (e.g., "攻击作为可插\n拔中间件"), creating a crowded column. The 4-bullet column appears tighter than the diagram.

**new-08 (场景库) — SPECIAL FOCUS**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Finding-style frametitle ("场景库覆盖三类道路几何，评价分四层指标") — excellent, this is the strongest frametitle in the deck. Booktabs table with bold values (54, 7, 8, 0.05s) — correct. "评价口径" block with dark-blue title strip — clean.
- Issue: There is a large blank region above the table — the table appears in the middle-lower half of the content area, leaving roughly 25% of content height blank above it. This dead space makes the slide feel poorly filled.
- Density: table (4 rows) + block (2 sentences). No violation.
- Hierarchy: Yes. Table rows with bold numerics draw the eye; block summarizes at bottom.

**new-09 (静态攻击实验设计)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Correct frametitle, correct block. Blue numbered enumerate with 4 items inside. "攻击类型" block below the numbered list contains three items on one line separated by bullets — functional but the block body is a single dense line with no visual breathing room.
- Density: Sub-header "实验目的" + "研究步骤" in plain colored text above the enumerate, then two blocks. Content is borderline heavy.
- Hierarchy: Yes, the numbered steps dominate.
- Issue: "实验目的" and "研究步骤" labels are again plain text (not inside a block), inconsistent with the pattern established in other slides where key labels are block-wrapped. Line "研究步骤" is not even visible as a separate label — it sits between the "实验目的" text and the enumeration.

**new-10 (静态结果) — SPECIAL FOCUS**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Booktabs table with bold values (0.51, 0.021, 5.0 for 弯道; 1.74, 0.057, 15.3 for 路口) — bold correctly applied to per-row min/max. Short gray italic footnote below table. "结论" block with dark-blue title strip and bold key phrase. All elements present.
- Density: table + footnote + block. No violation.
- Hierarchy: Bold numerics in table immediately draw attention, block confirms. Yes.
- Issue: The column header row shows "弯道 直道 路口" — the instruction says bold for 弯道 and 路口 (as they are the min/max), but the column headers themselves are NOT bolded — only the data values are bold. This is correct per the brief. Clean at distance. OK.
- Minor issue: the table is horizontally centered but left-aligned with wide left margin — the "攻击类型" column runs far left while numerics are center-right, creating a slightly awkward horizontal weight distribution. Not a critical defect.

**new-11 (防护回放) — SPECIAL FOCUS, RED CHECK**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Correct frametitle. Booktabs table, conclusion block.
- CRITICAL COLOR CHECK — "25.0%" and "未抑制": In the IMU row, the "防护后" column shows "25.0%" in RED (visually confirmed — clearly a different color from the black/gray of other numbers, distinctly red/crimson). The "综合" column shows "未抑制" also in RED. Both are unambiguously red at 180 DPI. PASS.
- The "15.4%" in GNSS 渐进漂移 row appears in bold dark (black/dark-blue), and "0.0%" in GNSS 恒定偏置 row is bold black — these are NOT red, which is correct (only the un-suppressed IMU row should be red).
- Density: table (3 rows × 4 cols) + footnote + block. No violation.
- Hierarchy: The red IMU row pops visually and the conclusion block confirms. Yes, clear in under 3 seconds.

**new-12 (动态攻击设计)**
- Style match: Correct frametitle. Diagram (3-node cycle: PPO策略 → CARLA闭环 → 奖励 → back) sits alone above block. "奖励设计" block with bold inline "幅度初始化". Clean.
- Density: one diagram + one block. No violation.
- Hierarchy: Yes.
- Issue: The diagram circle-flow layout is clear but the "奖励：失效 + 隐蔽 – 检测" box is notably smaller than the other two nodes, and its text has a line break that creates uneven sizing. Minor visual imbalance.

**new-13 (动态结果) — SPECIAL FOCUS**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Booktabs table, conclusion block.
- 13.8% row: The "屏蔽诊断维度补充" row is rendered in italic gray — visually clearly lighter/grayed vs the main black rows above. The 13.8% value is also in italic. PASS — the row is visibly grayed.
- Density: table (3 rows) + footnote + block. No violation.
- Hierarchy: The bold "50.1%" in PPO主实验row immediately draws the eye. Block confirms. Yes.
- Issue: The frametitle "动态攻击：检测率从 99.4% 降至 50.1%" is an excellent finding-style title. The column header row has "检测率 严重失效率 备注" — all in normal weight, booktabs style correct. Good.

**new-14 (动态行为) — SPECIAL FOCUS**
- Style match: PARTIAL. The two-column layout with one block per column, each containing a sparkline-style chart plus a caption label below, works reasonably.
- Charts: both show two lines in distinct colors (dark blue = 攻击幅度, red/salmon = 轨迹误差). Small legends inside each chart ("–攻击幅度 –轨迹误差") in the chart area.
- Density: 2 blocks, each self-contained. Below the blocks, a "共同点" paragraph with bold "CUSUM 累积门槛" inline. No enumeration overload.
- Hierarchy: The two charts provide immediate visual contrast. "共同点" label draws the shared insight. Yes.
- Issue: The "共同点：" label below the blocks appears as plain text (blue bold label + plain body), NOT inside a block wrapper, inconsistent with the block-for-conclusions pattern used on pages 10, 11, 13, 15, 16. This is a style inconsistency.
- Issue: The legend text in the mini charts ("–攻击幅度 –轨迹误差") is very small at slide scale. At projection distance this will likely be illegible. The sparklines themselves are sufficient to convey the trend, but legend labels risk being too tiny.

**new-15 (防护代价) — SPECIAL FOCUS, RED CHECK + MATH GLYPH**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Frametitle: "防护代价：能检测，未必能安全恢复" — no broken math glyph visible. The colon is a normal ASCII colon, text is clean. PASS on math glyph.
- RED CHECK — "未抑制": The IMU row "综合" column shows "未抑制" in RED. Visually confirmed — it is the same red as on page 11. PASS.
- Additional red check: The "25.0% → 25.0%" in the "严重失效降幅" column for IMU row — the "25.0%" values are in dark text (not red), which is correct per the design (only "未抑制" is the red highlight).
- Footnote: a two-line italic gray footnote below the table. The second line "──这是检测覆盖增强，不代表完整防护能力" uses an em-dash correctly. Clean.
- Density: table (3 rows) + 2-line footnote + block. On the edge but acceptable.
- Hierarchy: Yes. The "×" in IMU row and red "未抑制" pop immediately. Block confirms.

**new-16 (总结) — SPECIAL FOCUS**
- Style match: PARTIAL. "核心贡献" block fills the upper portion with the hero statement in bold italics ("检测—拒绝—停车—任务") inside double quotes. Below the block, a thin gray rule separates it from two bullet points acting as supporting detail.
- Issue: The "核心贡献" block body is VERY dense — four lines of text including the main claim AND the supporting 主要发现 paragraph, all crammed inside one block. The block text wraps to 4+ lines; this is too dense for a hero block. The main takeaway sentence is buried in the middle of a paragraph. A hero block should have ONE punchy sentence; the 主要发现 paragraph should be outside the block or in a second lighter block.
- Issue: The thin rule and the two supporting bullets below it ("构建CARLA闭环…" and "在54实例/7地图…") are present as expected. However both bullet items exceed 18 Chinese characters per line (each runs ~25–30 chars including Latin). Density violation.
- Hierarchy: The bold-quoted "检测—拒绝—停车—任务" is the intended hero phrase but it is not the FIRST thing the eye lands on — the block title "核心贡献" competes, and the dense paragraph body delays comprehension. Marginally No for the 3-second test.

**new-17 (当前局限明确，后续路径已规划)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Two side-by-side blocks ("当前不足" / "后续工作"), each with the dark-blue title strip. Symmetric layout, 3 bullets each. The "备用定位源" inline text at the end of the last bullet is in blue bold, consistent with the inline emphasis pattern.
- Density: 3 bullets per block, all readable lengths. No violation.
- Hierarchy: The two-block split makes the takeaway self-evident. Yes.
- Issue: The two blocks appear directly adjacent with very little gutter between them (the gap is narrower than the padding inside). At projection this may appear as one wide merged box.

**new-18 (谢谢)**
- Style match: Correct. Matches ref-23 exactly in structure.
- Hierarchy: Yes.

- new-11: "25.0%" (IMU 防护后) — RED. CONFIRMED. PASS.
- new-11: "未抑制" (IMU 综合) — RED. CONFIRMED. PASS.
- new-15: "未抑制" (IMU 综合) — RED. CONFIRMED. PASS.
- new-15: Math glyph in title — NO broken glyph. PASS.

## Concrete Fix List (Severity Order)

**1. CRITICAL — new-16: Hero block is too dense; supporting paragraph must be extracted.**
The "核心贡献" block contains both the hero claim and a 主要发现 paragraph (~60 chars, 4 lines). Split into: (a) block body = one short bold sentence only; (b) 主要发现 paragraph either outside the block as plain indented text or in a second lighter block. The two bottom bullets also exceed 18 chars/line and must be shortened or broken into sub-lines.

**2. HIGH — new-16: Supporting bullets violate 18-char/line limit.**
Both bullets below the rule are ~25–30 Chinese chars per line. Shorten to ≤18 chars by splitting into sub-items or rewording. Example: "构建 CARLA 闭环可复现框架 / 含攻击中间件与运行时防护" as two sub-lines.

**3. HIGH — new-04: Excessive dead space above the diagram.**
The diagram sits in the lower 60% of the content area, leaving ~35% of blank vertical space above it. Either shift diagram upward toward the frametitle or add a one-line "研究意义" sub-header above the diagram to fill the visual gap.

**4. HIGH — new-14: "共同点" conclusion not wrapped in a block.**
Unlike every other content slide, the key takeaway on new-14 appears as loose plain text ("共同点：…") without a block wrapper. Wrap in a rounded "结论" or "共同点" block with dark-blue title strip, consistent with pages 10–13, 15–16.

**5. MEDIUM — new-05 and new-09: Floating sub-headers ("已有基础" / "仍待解决" / "实验目的" / "研究步骤") are stylistically orphaned.**
These plain colored text labels are not inside blocks and look visually stranded. Either promote them to block titles (each wrapping their subordinate bullets) or demote them to bold inline text on the first bullet.

**6. MEDIUM — new-08 and new-04: Dead space above the booktabs table / diagram.**
On new-08, roughly 25% of content area is blank above the table. Pull the table toward the top of the content region or add the frametitle-finding label as a second line inside the frametitle bar.

**7. MEDIUM — new-14: Sparkline legends are illegibly small.**
The "–攻击幅度 –轨迹误差" legends inside the mini charts will be unreadable at projection distance. Move the legend below each chart as a caption line, or increase font size to at least 7pt equivalent.

**8. MEDIUM — new-17: Block gutter too narrow between "当前不足" and "后续工作".**
Add \hspace or increase columnsep so the two adjacent blocks have a visible gap (at least 0.5em) rather than appearing merged.

**9. LOW — new-03: Pipeline diagram ("场景 → 攻击 → 防护 → 闭环结果") has no visual container.**
The four boxes float between the two content blocks with no background rectangle or bounding frame. Add a light-gray background tcolorbox behind the pipeline or at minimum a thin-border white-fill outer rectangle, consistent with the TikZ container style used on new-04 and new-06.

**10. LOW — new-01: Footer separator is "|" (pipe) instead of reference style.**
ref-01 uses a double-space layout. The "|" is not wrong but breaks consistency with the reference. Change "答辩人: 王致尧 | 指导教师:" to match ref-01's em-space or thin-space separator.

**11. LOW — new-12: Diagram node size imbalance.**
The "奖励" node in the PPO cycle diagram is smaller than the other two nodes. Make all three nodes the same minimum-width box.

**Round 2 Revision Verification**

**new-04 "完整链路：" sub-line** — VERIFIED. The blue bold "完整链路：" label with the full pipeline description appears above the diagram, filling the previous dead space cleanly.

**new-12 4-node equal-sized PPO diagram** — NOT VERIFIED. The diagram has 4 nodes but they are visibly unequal in size: "观测 状态/防护统计/攻击历史" is roughly twice the height of "PPO 策略" and "CARLA 闭环 (防护开启)", and "奖励 失效+隐蔽-检测" is also oversized. The nodes are not equal-sized.

**new-14 "共同点" in \block{} + \scriptsize legends** — VERIFIED. "共同点" is rendered inside a dark-header block consistent with other slides, and the sparkline color legends ("攻击幅度 轨迹误差") are visibly small (scriptsize-equivalent). The left-right column gutter between the two panels is present and clear.

**new-16 核心贡献 block with one sentence, 主要发现 outside above rule** — VERIFIED. The block contains one sentence. "主要发现" with two short bullets sits outside/above the rule, and two supporting bullets appear below the rule in condensed form.

**new-17 visible gutter + condensed bullets (<=14 chars)** — PARTIALLY VERIFIED. The gutter between the two columns is visible. However, the right column "后续工作" has one bullet that wraps to a second line ("引入备用定位源（VO、高精 / 地图）"), indicating the text exceeds the 14-char condensed target. Left column bullets are short.

---

**Production Readiness: Yes, with one note**

The deck is production-ready for an undergrad defense. Content is logically sequenced, all key findings are stated with quantified numbers, honest negative findings are acknowledged (IMU attack not suppressed, PPO stability limited), and no overclaiming is present.

**One potentially embarrassing issue:** new-12 PPO closed-loop diagram — the "观测" node is roughly 2x taller than the other three. This asymmetry looks like a layout bug mid-presentation and may draw a committee question about whether the diagram is intentional.

**Style match score vs reference: 8/10.** The HUST Beamer theme, color palette, block structure, footer, and typography are consistent throughout; deductions for the unequal PPO nodes on new-12 and one wrapped bullet on new-17 that breaks the visual rhythm.

> DEVELOPER

You are a senior typographer / design critic auditing a defense-grade Beamer deck. Apply the HIGHEST standard. Use your multimodal vision — Read each PNG as an image; do not skip or skim.

NEW DECK (18 pages at 200 DPI):
/tmp/slide-png/new-01.png … /tmp/slide-png/new-18.png

REFERENCE (visual gold standard, 23 pages):
/tmp/slide-png/ref-01.png … /tmp/slide-png/ref-23.png — sample 6+ pages to internalize.

The deck has already been through 2 review rounds and matches the reference at the structural level (Madrid + whale colortheme, no orange, booktabs, bare blocks). Now I need DEEP POLISH — the kind a typographer would do before printing for a committee.

Apply these strict criteria, page by page:

1. **Vertical balance**: every page must fill the visual canvas. No empty thirds. No floating-low content. No floating-high titles. The visual mass should distribute across the body area.

2. **Single focal point**: a viewer at projection distance must identify the page's ONE message in ≤ 2 seconds. All other elements should support, not compete.

3. **Visual rhythm**: across the 18 pages, similar types of pages (e.g. the 5 table pages, the 4 diagram pages) should have consistent rhythm — same vertical placement, same spacing above/below tables.

4. **Bullet discipline**: NO line wraps in any bullet. Every bullet must fit one line. (Look hard — even one wrapped char is a failure.)

5. **Block usage discipline**: when a page has multiple "labels" (e.g. P5 "已有基础" / "仍待解决"; P9 "实验目的" / "研究步骤") — ALL labels must use the same primitive (all blocks, OR all bold sub-headers — never mixed within a page).

6. **Diagram quality**: nodes at uniform size; arrows consistent thickness; spacing equal; labels at consistent distance.

7. **Frametitle phrasing**: every title a finding, not a topic. ≤ 22 Chinese chars. No colons followed by topic noun phrases like "研究对象：..." UNLESS the second part is itself a finding.

8. **Footnote / supplementary text**: italic gray below tables/charts. Consistent style across pages 7, 10, 11, 13, 15.

9. **Color economy**: at most 3 colors per page — black body, dark blue (theme), red emphasis. Every other use is wrong.

10. **End-of-frame whitespace**: bottom padding above the footer should be visually balanced with top padding below the frametitle.

For EACH page (1-18), give:
- ONE-LINE verdict (POLISHED / NEEDS-WORK / BROKEN)
- IF needs work: specific concrete fix (with exact what-to-change instruction)

Then top of report: name the 5 most impactful improvements, ranked.

Be RUTHLESS. If something looks even slightly amateur, flag it. Aim for the standard of a published academic conference keynote, not a student slide deck.

Word budget: 700.

I now have a complete picture of all 18 slides and the reference standard. Here is the audit.

---

## TOP 5 MOST IMPACTFUL IMPROVEMENTS (ranked)

**1. Eliminate the dead-white upper thirds on pages 2, 4, 6, 9, 10, 11, 13, 15, 17.** The frametitle sits at top, then a full 35-40% blank white gap, then content begins. Reference slides have the content starting ~1.5 lines below the frametitle bar. Use `\vspace{-1em}` or reduce `\frametitle` top-skip. This is the single most visually damaging pattern.

**2. Fix all line-wrap violations in body text blocks (pages 3, 4, 6, 8, 12, 14, 15, 16).** The "本文切入点" block on p.3, the "研究意义" block on p.4, the block text on p.6, p.8, p.12, p.13, p.14, p.15, p.16 all wrap mid-sentence. Every block's body text should either be shortened, use a smaller font size, or have its container widened so each sentence fits on one visual unit.

**3. Resolve the block/header mixing inconsistency on pages 5 and 9.** On p.5, "已有基础" and "仍待解决" are plain bold colored sub-headers, but "本文回答的三个问题" is a dark blue block — three primitives on one page. On p.9, "实验目的:" is a bold-colored inline label while "研究步骤" is a subheader and "攻击类型" is a block. Pick one primitive system and apply it universally.

**4. Kill the colon-topic-phrase frametitles on pages 3, 4, 6, 7, 9.** "研究背景：仿真测试的必要性" is a topic, not a finding. "技术路线：从平台搭建到防护评价" is a topic. Replace with finding-phrasing: e.g., p.3 → "真实道路测试成本高，仿真不可或缺"; p.4 → "攻击注入贯穿定位到执行的完整闭环"; p.7 → "平台以中间件解耦攻击与防护". Pages 10, 11, 13, 15 already do this correctly — apply the same discipline to all.

**5. Normalize vertical positioning of tables across pages 8, 10, 11, 13, 15.** On p.10 the table is centered very high; on p.15 it is higher still; on p.8 it also floats high. All five table slides should position the top rule at the same fractional height (approximately 30% from top of content area). Currently each table sits at a different vertical, breaking visual rhythm across the deck's densest section.

---

## PAGE-BY-PAGE VERDICTS

**P1 (Title)** — NEEDS-WORK. The title block sits in the upper half and the name/date cluster in the middle, leaving the bottom third entirely empty. In the reference, the same content uses slightly more generous spacing below the blue box and the white space is more evenly split top/bottom. Add `\vfill` symmetry or push names down ~0.5em.

**P2 (TOC)** — NEEDS-WORK. Top third is dead white after the frametitle bar. The four numbered items occupy only the middle band. Collapse the gap: reduce `\vspace` after frametitle to ~0.3em. The numbered bubbles are blue/legible — that's fine.

**P3 (Background)** — BROKEN. Two violations: (a) title uses colon-topic structure; (b) the "本文切入点" block body wraps onto a second line ("防护开启后的变化。" alone on line 2), creating an awkward widow. Rewrite to fit one line, or reduce font one point.

**P4 (Research Object)** — BROKEN. Three violations: (a) colon-topic title; (b) large dead white gap between frametitle and content; (c) "研究意义" block body wraps to two lines. Also: "完整链路:" + colored bold text outside any block is a fourth label primitive inconsistent with the block below it. Convert the inline "完整链路:" annotation to match block style.

**P5 (Three Problems)** — BROKEN. Block-discipline failure: "已有基础" and "仍待解决" use colored bold sub-headers (no block), while "本文回答的三个问题" uses a dark block — three incompatible primitives on one slide. Promote all three labels to blocks, or demote all to bold sub-headers. Large top gap persists.

**P6 (Technical Roadmap)** — NEEDS-WORK. Title uses colon-topic structure. The "阶段说明" block body wraps to three lines ("PSO 结果是本文实验设置 / 下的候选最优参数，不作为全局最优证明。"). Compress to two lines max or tighten to one semantic unit.

**P7 (Platform)** — NEEDS-WORK. Title is a colon-topic phrase. The right-column bullets all wrap mid-item ("CARLA 0.9.15 / 同步模式", "攻击作为可插 / 拔中间件", etc.) — four of four bullets break across lines, violating bullet discipline criterion. Shorten or bump font down 1pt. The footnote in italic is correct style.

**P8 (Scenario Library)** — NEEDS-WORK. Table floats in the upper 45% of content area with a large blank below; "评价口径" block is pushed low. Both items in the block ("严重失效判定:" and "防护四层指标:") use bold-red inline labels inside the block body — this is a mixed primitive (block + bold-colored inline labels inside). Text also wraps. Move table up slightly, add \medskip, rewrite block text to remove the inline bold-red labels and instead treat each as a single sentence.

**P9 (Static Attack Design)** — BROKEN. Block-discipline: "实验目的" is an inline bold+color label with a colon, "研究步骤" is a plain bold section header, "攻击类型" is a dark block — three different primitives on one slide. Massive dead white gap (~40%) between frametitle and first element. Standardize all to blocks.

**P10 (Static Attack Results)** — POLISHED. Title is a genuine finding. Table is booktabs, footnote italic. Minor: the blank gap above the table is slightly oversized (~3cm); tighten by 0.5em to match p.11.

**P11 (Defense Replay)** — POLISHED. Title is a finding. Table + footnote + conclusion block laid out consistently with p.10. The red emphasis on "25.0%" and "未抑制" is correctly economical. One micro-issue: footnote line is long and barely fits — no actual wrap visible, acceptable.

**P12 (PPO Design)** — NEEDS-WORK. The RL loop diagram's four nodes are not equal in width: "CARLA 闭环（防护开启）" node is significantly wider than the other three. Standardize to equal minimum width. "设计要点" block body text wraps across three lines including an awkward break after the semicolon. Restructure as two lines max.

**P13 (Dynamic Attack Results)** — NEEDS-WORK. The third row (屏蔽诊断维度补充 / 13.8% / 100%) is rendered in gray italic to signal it is non-primary, but this creates a color economy violation: we now have black body, blue, red bold, AND gray italic simultaneously in the table, plus a separate italic footnote below. Simplify: keep the supplementary row in normal gray text without italic (italic is reserved for the footnote), or move it to the footnote entirely. Top gap too large.

**P14 (Dynamic Evasion Modes)** — POLISHED. Two-column block layout is visually balanced. Sparkline-style charts are equal size and aligned. "共同点" block at bottom is correctly weighted. The caption text under each chart ("全程低幅扰动→误差缓慢累积") fits one line each. Good rhythm. Only flaw: blocks have a slight drop-shadow/fill color difference between the top panels and the "共同点" block — should be uniform dark blue header style.

**P15 (Defense Cost)** — NEEDS-WORK. Footnote is two lines (wrapped) and uses italic — fine on its own, but the content is substantive enough that it should be a proper sub-note, not a footnote: "补充：角速率CUSUM在77次闭环测试中…" is a finding, not a caveat. Move inside the block or into a second thin block labeled "补充". Also, the em-dash continuation line "──这是检测覆盖增强…" looks typographically unpolished: use a proper \quad indent or parenthetical instead.

**P16 (Conclusion)** — NEEDS-WORK. Mixed structure: "核心贡献" has a block, "主要发现" uses a bold blue sub-header + bullets, then below a horizontal rule there are two more orphaned bullets with no header label. This creates three structural tiers with no visual logic. The two bottom bullets ("CARLA闭环+..." and "三类静态攻击...") have no label — they read as floating. Add a third block labeled "实验基础" or similar.

**P17 (Limitations)** — NEEDS-WORK. Extremely large dead-white upper gap — roughly the top 35% of the content area is empty before the two side-by-side blocks appear. The title is a genuine finding (good). The two-column block layout is clean but pushed far down. Raise the blocks by at least 2em. Also, "备用定位源：VO、高精地图" uses a colon-topic construction inside a bullet — marginal but acceptable at this level.

**P18 (Thank You)** — NEEDS-WORK. The "谢谢！" and subtitle float at exactly vertical center, leaving ~35% dead white above and ~30% below. For a closing slide this is conventional, but relative to the reference's closing slide this deck's ending feels cold. Add the school crest or a thin decorative rule to anchor the composition. Color is monochrome black on white — no theme color used at all. At minimum the subtitle should be in the theme dark blue.

**PAGE-BY-PAGE VERDICT**

---

**new-01 (Title) — MINOR**
The bottom ~35% of the canvas is dead white below "2026年5月"; push the date block down or add a thin HUST logo/rule at the bottom to close the gap.

---

**new-02 (TOC) — NEEDS-WORK**
Four items float in a void — large equal dead zones above item 1 and below item 4. The numbered circles (3D-sphere style) clash with the flat navy theme. Replace with flat-color numerals and compress vertical spacing so items occupy the center two-thirds of the canvas.

---

**new-03 (研究背景) — MINOR**
Solid content, but the pipeline diagram (场景→攻击→防护→闭环结果) nodes are visually narrower than the block labels above them; standardize node width to match block label height and add 2–3pt extra horizontal padding inside each node box.

---

**new-04 (研究对象) — NEEDS-WORK**
The "研究意义" block body text wraps to 2 lines ending in "差异。" — that is borderline acceptable — but the bottom ~40% of the slide is completely empty below the block, violating vertical balance standard 1; either enlarge the diagram or add a concise takeaway callout to fill the lower third.

---

**new-05 (现有问题) — MINOR**
Left block "已有基础" bullet "GNSS欺骗与IMU偏置已被验证" wraps to a second line inside the block; tighten to one line (e.g., "GNSS欺骗与IMU偏置已验证") to satisfy standard 3.

---

**new-06 (技术路线) — NEEDS-WORK**
Three issues compound: (1) diagram row 2 nodes (7结果分析, 6PPO训练, 5防护验证) are narrower than row 1 nodes — uniform width violated; (2) the italic PSO caveat line below the block is a third primitive style (neither block nor bullet), breaking standard 6 uniformity; (3) bottom ~30% empty. Move caveat inside the block as a parenthetical or footnote rule, and scale nodes to equal width.

---

**TOP 3 FIXES BY IMPACT**

1. **Vertical dead space (slides 1, 2, 4, 6)** — Four of six slides leave the bottom quarter to third entirely blank; this reads as unfinished at projection distance. Add anchoring elements (logo strip, callout, expanded diagram) to close the gap.

2. **Diagram node width inconsistency (slides 3, 6)** — Non-uniform box widths on the pipeline/phase diagrams destroy visual rhythm; set a single fixed `minimum width` in TikZ for all nodes across both slides.

3. **TOC sphere icons vs. flat theme (slide 2)** — The 3D gradient number badges are the only skeuomorphic element in the deck; replacing them with flat filled circles eliminates the style collision and unifies the visual language.

---

**PAGE-BY-PAGE VERDICTS**

**p7 — 实验平台 (平台以中间件解耦攻击与防护)**
VERDICT: NEEDS-WORK

Right-column bullets are NOT all single-line. Two bullets wrap: "CARLA 0.9.15 同 / 步模式" and "攻击为可插拔中间 / 件" and "全量记录轨迹/事 / 件" — three of four items wrap, failing Standard 3. The bottom third of the slide is empty white void (~30% of canvas), violating Standard 1. The italicized footer note floats mid-canvas with no visual anchor.

Fix: Shorten the four bullet strings so each fits one line at current font size (e.g., "CARLA 0.9.15 同步模式" → abbreviate or reduce font by 1pt); raise the two-block group vertically to close the bottom gap.

---

**p8 — 场景库 (场景库覆盖三类道路几何，评价分四层指标)**
VERDICT: MINOR

Table vertical placement is high (upper third); the two blocks sit in the lower half — this asymmetric split creates visual top-heaviness with a large whitespace gap between table and blocks. The "防护四层指标" block body wraps to 2 lines ("检测触发 / 测量拒绝 / 停车响应 / 任务完成——逐项分别统计。") — technically one long run-on line that breaks at the slash cluster. Blue hyperlink color ("逐项分别统计") is outside the approved color palette (navy/black/red only). Bottom third is largely empty.

Fix: Remove the blue hyperlink color on "逐项分别统计" — use red emphasis instead — and compress vertical gap between table and block pair by ~8mm to tighten bottom whitespace.

---

**p9 — 静态攻击实验设计 (静态攻击：网格扫描确定区间，PSO 精细搜索)**
VERDICT: NEEDS-WORK

Critical primitive inconsistency: 研究步骤 uses a full \block with numbered list items; 实验目的 uses a \block with body text (consistent); but 攻击类型 renders as a \block header with **plain body text and no inner border or fill** — the block body area has no background shading, making it visually a hybrid (labeled header, raw text body). This violates Standard 6. The three items "GNSS 恒定偏置 / GNSS 渐进漂移 / IMU 航向偏置" are plain flush-left text inside the 攻击类型 block, styled differently from the 研究步骤 numbered list. Bottom third is empty (~25% blank).

Fix: Give 攻击类型 the same block body treatment as 实验目的 (dark header bar + filled body background), and either use a bulleted list or plain paragraph consistently across all three right-column blocks.

---

**p10 — 静态攻击结果 (静态攻击：道路几何决定有效阈值)**
VERDICT: MINOR

Table vertical placement: top-rule starts approximately at 28% from top of content area — noticeably higher than p11's table (which starts ~30%). The gap between footnote italic line and the 结论 block is large (~15% of canvas height). Bottom third again empty. The 结论 block body wraps to 2 lines — acceptable per Standard 4. Red and bold used correctly. No orange. Table/footnote rhythm is close to p11 but not pixel-matched.

Fix: Lower the table by ~5mm to align top-rule visually with p11, closing the footnote-to-block gap simultaneously.

---

**p11 — 防护回放 (防护回放：风险被压缩，但并非全部)**
VERDICT: MINOR

Table is positioned slightly lower than p10 (top-rule ~30% down content area vs p10's ~28%) — a measurable desync breaking cross-slide rhythm (Standard 10). The footnote italic text is significantly longer than p10's footnote, causing it to wrap to two lines vs p10's one line — inconsistent footnote style. "未抑制" in red bold within the table is correct usage. The 结论 block body wraps to 2 lines — borderline but acceptable. Bottom third empty.

Fix: Align top-rule of this table to match p10 exactly (raise ~2mm), and trim the footnote to single-line length to match p10's footnote format.

---

**p12 — 动态攻击设计 PPO (PPO 在防护开启环境中训练)**
VERDICT: NEEDS-WORK

The 4 cycle nodes are NOT uniform width. "CARLA 闭环（防护开启）" is visibly wider (~2.2×) than "观测", "PPO 策略", and "奖励" — destroying the cycle's visual symmetry (Standard 7). The diagram occupies only the top 40% of the canvas; the two blocks below are vertically misaligned with where the eye expects them after the diagram. The "对照设置" block body wraps to 2 lines ("策略每步输出注入幅度；静态搜索 / 结果只作幅度初始化。") — marginally acceptable, but "幅度初始化" blue-link color violates palette (Standard 8). Bottom third empty.

Fix: Normalize all 4 TikZ nodes to the same `minimum width` (use the widest label's width for all), and recolor "幅度初始化" to red.

---

**TOP 3 FIXES BY IMPACT**

1. **p12 node width uniformity** — The non-uniform cycle diagram is the single most visually damaging defect across all 6 pages; a misshapen cycle on the PPO slide directly undermines comprehension of the training loop at projection distance. Set all 4 nodes to `minimum width=3.2cm` (or whatever fits "CARLA 闭环（防护开启）").

2. **p7 right-column bullet wraps** — Three of four bullets wrap, which is the most blatant violation of Standard 3 in this range; it has survived three review rounds and must be fixed by shortening strings or reducing font 1pt within the block.

3. **p9 攻击类型 block primitive mismatch** — The mixed primitive (dark header + unstyled body) breaks Standard 6 and creates a visually jarring inconsistency on the slide that introduces the three attack types — a foundational concept repeated across p10 and p11. It must be rendered as a proper \block identical in structure to 实验目的.

> AGENT

---

**P13 — 动态攻击结果**
VERDICT: MINOR
The 13.8% row is correctly grayed and visually subordinate. The footnote italic treatment is consistent with earlier slides. One flaw: the bottom third is nearly empty — the 结论 block sits mid-canvas with ~30% dead white space below it, violating vertical balance.
Fix: raise the 结论 block upward or add \vfill distribution to close the bottom gap.

---

**P14 — 动态攻击行为**
VERDICT: MINOR
The two top diagram blocks and the bottom 共同点 block all carry the same dark-navy header chrome — uniform. Color legends (blue/red lines) are readable. The flaw: there is a wide blank band between frametitle and the first block pair (~15% of canvas), and another gap below 共同点 (~15%), making the content feel adrift rather than anchored.
Fix: reduce the top spacer and bottom padding so blocks fill the canvas more symmetrically.

---

**P15 — 防护代价**
VERDICT: POLISHED
Hero conclusion is instant: "未抑制" in red, bold ✗ in the table, and the 结论 block leading sentence in bold red all converge on the same message within 2 seconds. The two-column bottom block split (结论 + 补充) is clean and proportional. No wrapping issues.

---

**P16 — 总结**
VERDICT: NEEDS-WORK
Three critical failures. (1) The frametitle has no dark-navy bar — it renders in plain body style against a white background, losing the consistent chrome of every other slide (no \frametitle block decoration). (2) The three blocks (核心贡献 / 主要发现 / 实验基础) are vertically unbalanced — 核心贡献 text wraps to 2 lines while 实验基础 is only 1 line, so block heights differ noticeably and compete visually. (3) 主要发现 uses a bullet-list primitive inside a block while the other two use plain prose — mixed primitives within a single slide.
Fix: normalize all three blocks to the same \block chrome with consistent internal prose style (no mixed bullet/prose), and equalize text lengths so block heights are comparable.

---

**P17 — 不足与展望**
VERDICT: NEEDS-WORK
The two column blocks occupy only the top ~45% of the canvas; the bottom half is a dead white void. At projection distance this reads as an unfinished slide. The frametitle is finding-style and the block chrome is uniform — those are fine. The color highlight on "备用定位源" is navy-blue, which blends into the block header color and reduces pop.
Fix: stretch blocks vertically or add a third anchor element (e.g., a single-line closing statement block) to occupy the bottom third, and change "备用定位源" emphasis to red.

---

**P18 — 致谢**
VERDICT: MINOR
"谢谢" is large and in theme navy — not cold. The affiliation line provides a subtle anchor. However, the three text elements (谢谢 / 欢迎各位老师批评指正 / affiliation) are grouped in the center-lower third, leaving the top ~40% completely blank. The visual center of gravity is too low relative to the optical center of the frame.
Fix: shift the text cluster up ~10–12% so it sits at the optical center, balancing top and bottom negative space equally.

---

**TOP 3 FIXES BY IMPACT**

1. **P16 — Restore frametitle bar and unify block primitives** (HIGHEST): The missing dark-navy frametitle chrome breaks deck-wide visual consistency on the most important summary slide, and mixing bullet/prose primitives inside blocks signals structural disorder to the committee.

2. **P17 — Fill the bottom void** (HIGH): A half-empty canvas on the 不足与展望 slide reads as unfinished; with content confined to the top half, vertical balance fails the most basic design standard for projection distance.

3. **P13 / P18 — Close bottom dead-space gaps** (MEDIUM): Both slides leave roughly 25–30% white below the last element; a consistent \vfill or block repositioning across both eliminates the "content floating near top" impression that undermines polish accumulated over three review rounds.

**TOP 2 MOST-IMPACTFUL REMAINING FIXES (pages 1-6):**

1. **P2 (Outline) + P4 (Research Object): ~35-40% of canvas is empty white below the last item.** Both slides have genuine dead-space bands at the bottom that would fail a conference-keynote inspection. The `[t]` + `\vfill` fix apparently did not propagate to these two frames.

2. **P4 (Research Object): only 2 elements** (diagram + one block) with the diagram floating in a large empty upper zone. The canvas is only ~50% utilized. This is the single weakest slide in the set.

---

**P1 — Title**
VERDICT: POLISHED
The navy banner, subtitle, author/advisor line, institution, and date are well-proportioned. The bottom dead band is standard for a HUST title frame and matches the reference. No action needed.

---

**P2 — 汇报提纲**
VERDICT: NEEDS-WORK
Four numbered items cluster in the center-upper third; approximately 35% of the canvas below item 4 is blank white. The reference (ref-02) shows the same template but with 5 items that better fill the space. Fix: add vertical spacing between items via `\vspace{\fill}` between each entry, or increase `\itemsep` so the four items distribute across the full content area.

---

**P3 — 研究背景**
VERDICT: POLISHED
Three-element layout (top block with bullets / 4-node flow diagram / bottom block) fills the canvas cleanly. Bullets are 1 line each, no wraps. Frametitle is a crisp finding statement under 22 chars. Color palette clean (navy/black/blue-bold). Matches reference density standard.

---

**P4 — 研究对象**
VERDICT: NEEDS-WORK
The 5-node pipeline diagram occupies the upper half alone; the 研究意义 block sits below with a visible ~30% dead zone at the bottom. Only 2 structural elements on the slide — it reads sparse against the reference standard. Fix: add a second explanatory block (e.g., a 本文聚焦 callout or a brief 3-item annotation row below the diagram nodes) to consume the dead zone, or expand the existing block's body to 2 full lines.

---

**P5 — 现有问题与三个研究问题**
VERDICT: POLISHED
Three-block structure (已有基础 / 仍待解决 side-by-side, then 本文回答的三个问题 full-width below) fills the canvas well. The three numbered research questions are each single-line with highlighted key terms. Block primitive style is consistent across all three. No wraps detected. Minor note: the side-by-side top row has a slight height asymmetry (right block body is shorter), but not visually disruptive at presentation distance.

---

**P6 — 技术路线**
VERDICT: MINOR
The 7-node two-row flow is clear and the 阶段说明 block below is correctly populated including the PSO caveat in italic. However, there is a perceptible ~25% dead band below the 阶段说明 block. Fix: increase the block's `\vspace` padding or add `\vfill` after it so the block expands to anchor to the bottom margin, consuming the dead zone.

**TOP 2 MOST-IMPACTFUL REMAINING FIXES (pages 7-12):**

1. **P7 vertical balance** — the diagram + block cluster sits in the upper-center third; roughly 35% of the canvas below the 说明 block is dead white. The 说明 block needs to be pushed down (or the diagram cluster lifted) so content spans ~70% of the usable height.

2. **P12 TikZ node text kerning** — despite uniform `text width=3.4cm`, all four node labels render with visible inter-character spacing artifacts ("观 测", "PPO 策 略", "奖 励", "CARLA 闭 环"). This is the most visually unprofessional detail in the entire range and will be caught immediately by a technical audience.

---

**P7 — 实验平台**
VERDICT: MINOR
Fix: The entire diagram+block group occupies only the top 45% of the canvas; the 说明 block at bottom leaves ~25% dead white between it and the diagram. Add `\vspace` to distribute the gap, or anchor 说明 with `\vfill` above it so content fills to ~80% canvas height.

**P8 — 场景库与评价口径**
VERDICT: MINOR
Fix: The table floats with ~30% whitespace above it (no top anchor). Apply `\vspace*{-0.5em}` before the table or use `\vfill` strategy consistent with p10/p11 — the table on p8 sits noticeably lower on screen than the tables on p10/p11, breaking the shared visual rhythm required by criterion 6.

**P9 — 静态攻击实验设计**
VERDICT: MINOR
Fix: The 实验目的 block body wraps to 2 lines ("搜索最小有效静态攻击候选参数，/ 作为防护评估的输入。") which is within spec, but the 研究步骤 block is significantly taller than the two right-column blocks combined, leaving a large whitespace gap below 攻击类型. Align block heights by adding a 5th micro-bullet or using `\vfill` inside the right column.

**P10 — 静态攻击结果**
VERDICT: POLISHED
Table is top-anchored, footnote style matches p11, 结论 block is single finding, bold highlights effective.

**P11 — 防护回放**
VERDICT: POLISHED
Red "未抑制" cell is the correct single-color exception, 结论 block body wraps to exactly 2 lines, footnote style consistent with p10.

**P12 — 动态攻击设计 PPO**
VERDICT: NEEDS-WORK
Fix: All four TikZ node labels show letter-spaced text ("观 测", "PPO 策 略", "奖 励", "CARLA 闭 环 / （防 护 开 启）") — this is a `CJKspace` or `xeCJK` inter-character spacing artifact inside TikZ nodes. Add `\CJKsetecglue{}` inside the node style or wrap label text in `\mbox{}` to suppress the unwanted spacing. This is the single most visible rendering defect in the range.

129	    \begin{column}{0.485\textwidth}
130	      \begin{block}{研究意义}
131	        在仿真中观察定位攻击如何沿\bluehead{观测—估计—控制}传播到车辆轨迹。
132	      \end{block}
133	    \end{column}
134	    \hspace{0.02\textwidth}
135	    \begin{column}{0.485\textwidth}
136	      \begin{block}{研究边界}
137	        只研究\bluehead{定位链路}的攻击与防护，不涉及感知、决策与其他信息安全问题。
138	      \end{block}
139	    \end{column}
140	  \end{columns}
141	\end{frame}
142	
143	% ===== 5 现有问题与三个研究问题 =====
144	\begin{frame}
145	  \frametitle{现有研究多关注检测，告警之后的车辆状态评估不足}
146	  \vfill
147	  \begin{columns}[T,onlytextwidth]
148	    \begin{column}{0.485\textwidth}
149	      \begin{block}{已有基础}
150	        \begin{itemize}\setlength{\itemsep}{0.18em}
151	          \item CARLA 已能支撑闭环驾驶仿真
152	          \item GNSS 欺骗、IMU 偏置已被讨论
153	        \end{itemize}
154	      \end{block}
155	    \end{column}
156	    \hspace{0.02\textwidth}
157	    \begin{column}{0.485\textwidth}
158	      \begin{block}{尚未充分回答}
159	        \begin{itemize}\setlength{\itemsep}{0.18em}
160	          \item 不同场景下的最小有效幅度
161	          \item 防护告警之后的\bluehead{车辆实际状态}
162	        \end{itemize}
163	      \end{block}
164	    \end{column}
165	  \end{columns}
166	  \vfill
167	  \begin{block}{本文讨论的三个具体问题}
168	    \begin{enumerate}\setlength{\itemsep}{0.18em}
169	      \item 不同道路几何下，让车辆失效所需的\bluehead{最小攻击幅度}相差多少？
170	      \item 加入防护后，静态搜索得到的攻击边界还能代表\bluehead{真实风险}吗？
171	      \item 经过学习的\bluehead{动态攻击者}是否会改变上述评估？
172	    \end{enumerate}
173	  \end{block}
174	  \vfill
175	\end{frame}

290	\section{实验结果与分析}
291	%========================================================================
292	
293	% ===== 9 静态攻击实验设计 =====
294	\begin{frame}
295	  \frametitle{静态攻击：先网格扫描，再 PSO 精细搜索}
296	  \vfill
297	  \begin{block}{目的}
298	    为每类攻击找到能稳定触发严重失效的\bluehead{最小幅度}，作为后续防护回放的输入。
299	  \end{block}
300	  \vfill
301	  \begin{columns}[T,onlytextwidth]
302	    \begin{column}{0.485\textwidth}
303	      \begin{block}{步骤}
304	        \begin{enumerate}\setlength{\itemsep}{0.22em}
305	          \item 在参数空间做\bluehead{网格扫描}
306	          \item 识别候选\bluehead{失效区间}
307	          \item 在区间内用 \bluehead{PSO} 精细搜索
308	          \item 切换\bluehead{定位变体}做回放验证
309	        \end{enumerate}
310	      \end{block}
311	    \end{column}
312	    \hspace{0.02\textwidth}
313	    \begin{column}{0.485\textwidth}
314	      \begin{block}{攻击类型}
315	        \begin{itemize}\setlength{\itemsep}{0.22em}
316	          \item GNSS 恒定偏置
317	          \item GNSS 渐进漂移
318	          \item IMU 航向偏置
319	        \end{itemize}
320	      \end{block}
321	    \end{column}
322	  \end{columns}
323	  \vfill
324	\end{frame}
325	
326	% ===== 10 静态攻击结果 =====
327	\begin{frame}
328	  \frametitle{静态攻击：道路几何决定有效阈值}
329	  \vfill
330	  \begin{center}\small
331	  \begin{tabular}{@{}lccc@{}}
332	    \toprule
333	    攻击类型 & 弯道 & 直道 & 路口 \\
334	    \midrule
335	    GNSS 恒定偏置\;(m)        & \textbf{0.51} & 1.55 & \textbf{1.74} \\
336	    GNSS 渐进漂移\;(m/s)      & \textbf{0.021}& 0.032& \textbf{0.057}\\
337	    IMU 航向偏置\;($^{\circ}$)& \textbf{5.0}  & 11.0 & \textbf{15.3} \\
338	    \bottomrule
339	  \end{tabular}\par\vspace{0.3em}
340	  {\footnotesize\itshape\color{black!60}
341	  阈值 = 触发严重失效所需的最小攻击幅度；加粗 = 行内最小/最大。}
342	  \end{center}
343	  \vspace{0.8em}
344	  \begin{block}{结论}
345	    \bluehead{弯道阈值最小，路口最大；}三类攻击的排序一致，漂移攻击在不同定位变体之间复现最稳定。
346	  \end{block}
347	  \vfill
348	\end{frame}
349	
350	% ===== 11 防护回放 =====
351	\begin{frame}
352	  \frametitle{防护回放：GNSS 类被抑制，IMU 仍可造成失效}
353	  \vfill
354	  \begin{center}\small
355	  \begin{tabular}{@{}lcccl@{}}
356	    \toprule
357	    攻击类型 & 无防护 & 防护后 & 触发均值 & 综合 \\
358	    \midrule
359	    GNSS 恒定偏置 & 15.4\% & \textbf{0.0\%}  & 926 次 & 有效抑制 \\
360	    GNSS 渐进漂移 & 23.1\% & \textbf{15.4\%} & 322 次 & 部分抑制 \\
361	    IMU 航向偏置  & 25.0\% & \textcolor{red!75!black}{\textbf{25.0\%}} & 0 次   & \textcolor{red!75!black}{\textbf{未抑制}} \\
362	    \bottomrule
363	  \end{tabular}\par\vspace{0.3em}
364	  {\footnotesize\itshape\color{black!60}
365	  严重失效率（13 个场景统一回放）；触发均值为每回合 GNSS 防护触发次数。}
366	  \end{center}
367	  \vspace{0.8em}
368	  \begin{block}{结论}
369	    卡方/CUSUM 能挡住 GNSS 类攻击；\bluehead{IMU 航向偏置不进入 GNSS 检测视野}，失效率原样保留。
370	  \end{block}
371	  \vfill
372	\end{frame}

375	\begin{frame}

377	  \vfill
378	  \centering
379	  \begin{tikzpicture}[
380	    node distance=0.7cm and 1.4cm,
381	    box/.style={draw,line width=0.5pt,rounded corners=2pt,fill=white,

384	    lab/.style={font=\scriptsize,black!55}]

386	    \node[box,right=of obs] (policy) {PPO 策略};

395	    \node[lab,below] at ($(env)!0.5!(reward)$) {失效 / 检测};
396	    \node[lab,left] at ($(reward)!0.5!(obs)$) {奖惩反馈};
397	  \end{tikzpicture}

401	      \begin{block}{观测与奖励}

403	        \bluehead{奖励}\,$=$\,失效\,$+$\,隐蔽\,$-$\,被检测
404	      \end{block}
405	    \end{column}
406	    \hspace{0.02\textwidth}
407	    \begin{column}{0.485\textwidth}
408	      \begin{block}{对照设置}
409	        策略逐步决定攻击幅度；静态搜索结果只用于\bluehead{设置初始幅度}。
410	      \end{block}
411	    \end{column}
412	  \end{columns}
413	  \vfill
414	\end{frame}

416	% ===== 13 动态攻击结果 =====
417	\begin{frame}
418	  \frametitle{动态攻击：检测率降至 50.1\%，失效率仍 100\%}
419	  \vfill
420	  \begin{center}\small
421	  \begin{tabular}{@{}lccl@{}}
422	    \toprule
423	    设置 & 检测率 & 严重失效率 & 备注 \\
424	    \midrule
425	    无检测惩罚（基线对照） & 99.4\% & 100\% & 攻击全程被检测 \\
426	    PPO 主实验             & \textbf{50.1\%} & \textbf{100\%} & 4 路口场景\,/\,3 种子均值 \\
427	    \addlinespace[2pt]
428	    {\color{black!50}屏蔽诊断维度（补充实验）} & {\color{black!50}13.8\%} & {\color{black!50}100\%} & {\color{black!50}消融性验证} \\
429	    \bottomrule
430	  \end{tabular}\par\vspace{0.3em}
431	  {\footnotesize\itshape\color{black!60}
432	  检测率按时间步统计，不含停车响应；13.8\% 用于验证隐蔽性是否依赖诊断维度。}
433	  \end{center}
434	  \vspace{0.8em}
435	  \begin{block}{结论}
436	    PPO 能保持 \bluehead{100\% 严重失效率}的同时把被检测频率降低近一半；3 个随机种子之间差异较大，仍待进一步验证。
437	  \end{block}
438	  \vfill
439	\end{frame}
440

442	\begin{frame}
443	  \frametitle{动态攻击的两类典型行为模式}
444	  \vfill
445	  \begin{columns}[T,onlytextwidth]
446	    \begin{column}{0.485\textwidth}
447	      \begin{block}{持续微小注入}
448	        \centering
449	        {\scriptsize\color{black!70}
450	          \textcolor{structure.fg}{\rule[0.4ex]{0.9em}{0.7pt}}\,攻击幅度\quad
451	          \textcolor{red!75!black}{\rule[0.4ex]{0.9em}{0.7pt}}\,轨迹误差}\par

453	          \draw[black!30,line width=0.25pt,dashed] (0,1.0) -- (18,1.0);
454	          \draw[->,black!50,line width=0.4pt] (0,0) -- (18.3,0);
455	          \draw[->,black!50,line width=0.4pt] (0,0) -- (0,1.18);
456	          \draw[structure.fg,line width=0.7pt] plot[smooth]
457	            coordinates {(0,0.45) (3,0.55) (6,0.48) (9,0.58) (12,0.52) (15,0.60) (18,0.56)};
458	          \draw[red!75!black,line width=0.7pt] plot[smooth]
459	            coordinates {(0,0.10) (4,0.18) (8,0.32) (12,0.56) (16,0.88) (18,1.05)};
460	          \node[font=\tiny,black!60] at (16.5,-0.13) {时间};
461	        \end{tikzpicture}\par\vspace{0.15em}
462	        {\footnotesize 全程低幅扰动 → 误差缓慢累积}
463	      \end{block}
464	    \end{column}
465	    \hspace{0.02\textwidth}
466	    \begin{column}{0.485\textwidth}
467	      \begin{block}{隐蔽—爆发切换}
468	        \centering
469	        {\scriptsize\color{black!70}
470	          \textcolor{structure.fg}{\rule[0.4ex]{0.9em}{0.7pt}}\,攻击幅度\quad
471	          \textcolor{red!75!black}{\rule[0.4ex]{0.9em}{0.7pt}}\,轨迹误差}\par
472	        \begin{tikzpicture}[x=0.20cm,y=1.0cm]
473	          \draw[black!30,line width=0.25pt,dashed] (0,1.0) -- (18,1.0);
474	          \draw[->,black!50,line width=0.4pt] (0,0) -- (18.3,0);
475	          \draw[->,black!50,line width=0.4pt] (0,0) -- (0,1.18);
476	          \draw[structure.fg,line width=0.7pt] plot[smooth]
477	            coordinates {(0,0.28) (4,0.30) (8,0.32) (11,0.34) (12,0.78) (15,0.90) (18,0.86)};

479	            coordinates {(0,0.08) (5,0.10) (10,0.16) (12,0.28) (15,0.70) (18,1.10)};

481	        \end{tikzpicture}\par\vspace{0.15em}
482	        {\footnotesize 前段隐蔽 → 后段集中扰动 → 失效}
483	      \end{block}
484	    \end{column}
485	  \end{columns}
486	  \vfill
487	  \begin{block}{共同点}
488	    两类策略都把每步攻击幅度控制在 \bluehead{CUSUM 累积阈值}之下，由此规避检测，又能让横向误差最终越界。
489	  \end{block}
490	  \vfill
491	\end{frame}
492	
493	% ===== 15 防护代价 =====
494	\begin{frame}
495	  \frametitle{防护代价：能检测，未必能安全恢复}
496	  \vfill
497	  \begin{center}\small
498	  \begin{tabular}{@{}lccl@{}}
499	    \toprule
500	    攻击类型 & 是否被检测 & 严重失效降幅 & 综合 \\
501	    \midrule
502	    GNSS 恒定偏置 & $\checkmark$ & 15.4\%\;$\to$\;\textbf{0.0\%}  & 有效 \\
503	    GNSS 渐进漂移 & $\checkmark$ & 23.1\%\;$\to$\;\textbf{15.4\%} & 部分 \\
504	    IMU 航向偏置  & \textcolor{red!75!black}{\textbf{$\times$}} & 25.0\%\;$\to$\;\textbf{25.0\%} & \textcolor{red!75!black}{\textbf{未抑制}} \\
505	    \bottomrule
506	  \end{tabular}
507	  \end{center}
508	  \vfill
509	  \begin{columns}[T,onlytextwidth]
510	    \begin{column}{0.56\textwidth}
511	      \begin{block}{结论}
512	        \bluehead{防护检测到攻击，并不等于车辆已经回到安全状态。}因此静态搜索得到的攻击参数不能直接代表防护开启后的风险。
513	      \end{block}
514	    \end{column}
515	    \hspace{0.02\textwidth}
516	    \begin{column}{0.40\textwidth}
517	      \begin{block}{补充：角速率 CUSUM}
518	        77 次预设 IMU 姿态攻击中均能\bluehead{5\,s 内触发}，但仅增强检测覆盖，无法接管控制。
519	      \end{block}
520	    \end{column}
521	  \end{columns}
522	  \vfill
523	\end{frame}

Here is the audit:

---

**Page 1 (Title)** — ✅ Clean. Layout and wording are fine.

**Page 2 (TOC)** — 🔴 **Structure.** The four items float in a mostly empty frame with an enormous blank gap at the bottom (roughly 35% of the frame unused below item 4). The \vspace{1.0em} + \tableofcontents combination leaves no bottom anchor. Fix: add `\vfill` before and after `\tableofcontents`, or set a fixed `\setbeamersize` row-stretch so the four items are vertically centred in the content area rather than top-hugging.

**Page 3 (研究背景)** — 🔴 **Structure + AI wording.**
- The TikZ pipeline (场景→攻击→防护→闭环结果) floats as a bare naked row between two blocks, with no visual grouping — it reads as an orphaned element, not a step inside the argument. Consider wrapping it inside the upper block or giving it a thin shaded background bar.
- "本文做法" is the AI 本文 pattern. The block title "本文做法" can simply become "做法" — the frametitle already contextualises it.

**Page 4 (研究对象)** — 🔴 **TikZ + Structure.**
- The five pipeline boxes are noticeably squished: `node distance=0.42cm` is too tight, so "GNSS/IMU 观测" and "越界/碰撞\轨迹偏离" are close to colliding. The last box is also the widest, making the row visually unbalanced.
- The sub-labels ("传感链", "威胁面", …) are placed `below=2pt of` the nodes, which is extremely close and visually merges with the box border. Use `below=5pt` or `yshift=-2pt` on a `node[lab]` to give them breathing room.
- No AI wording found here.

**Page 5 (研究问题)** — 🔴 **AI wording + Structure.**
- "还能代表**真实风险**吗？" — "真实风险" is a classic AI hedging phrase. Change to: "加入防护后，静态边界还能反映攻击实际危害吗？"
- The layout is 3 blocks stacked with near-equal visual weight (两栏上方 + 全宽下方). The bottom "本文讨论的三个具体问题" block is twice the height of the two above it but carries the same dark-blue title bar. This works visually; the real issue is the title "本文讨论的三个具体问题" — the "本文" prefix is the AI 口癖. Shorten to "三个具体问题" or "待回答的三个问题".
- The two-column upper block is unbalanced: left block has 2 short bullets, right block also has 2 but one wraps ("GNSS 欺骗、IMU 偏置已被讨 / 论" wraps mid-word at "讨"). Fix: set left column to `0.52\textwidth` and right to `0.44\textwidth`, or reword to avoid the wrap.

**Page 6 (技术路线)** — 🔴 **TikZ + AI wording.**
- The "说明" block contains two sentences at different font sizes/weights with no visual separator — the italic footnote in `\footnotesize\itshape` reads as a caption pasted inside a block, which looks unpolished. Move the disclaimer outside the block as a plain `\footnotesize` line below it.
- "本研究设置下" in `PSO 结果是本研究设置下的候选最优` is the "本研究" AI pattern. Change to: "PSO 结果是当前参数设置下的候选最优".
- The S-bend pipeline (row 1 left-to-right, row 2 right-to-left) renders correctly but the vertical arrow from s4→s5 has no label, making the serpentine flow visually ambiguous at a glance. A tiny label "↓" or arrow annotation would help.

**Page 7 (实验平台)** — 🔴 **TikZ.**
- The feedback loop dashed line (`vehicle.west -| sensor.south`) draws down then left then up, producing a visually heavy L-path that extends beyond the left edge of GNSS/IMU. The `|-` routing makes it exit below sensor rather than re-entering cleanly from the left. Fix: use `\draw[loop] (vehicle.west) -- ++(-1.2,0) |- (sensor.south);` with an explicit offset so the return arc is tucked below the row without overshooting.
- "攻击 / 防护事件时间戳" in the 记录范围 block — the "戳" is cut off in the rendered slide (visible in the PNG as "时间戳" with no overflow, actually fine on inspection). No actual clipping here.
- Page is otherwise clean in wording.

**Page 8 (场景库)** — 🔴 **AI wording + Structure.**
- "检测触发 / 测量拒绝 / 停车响应 / 任务完成，**分别统计**。" — "分别统计" is the罗列式 AI 口癖 flagged in the brief. Change to: "检测触发、测量拒绝、停车响应、任务完成四项逐一量化。" or more natural: "四项指标在同一回合中独立记录。"
- The table floats in a large empty upper zone (same top-heavy issue as p2). The `\vfill` before `\begin{center}` pushes the table centre-ish, but the blank gap between frametitle and table still spans ~30% of slide height. Add a `\vspace{-0.5em}` after `\vfill` or reduce the first `\vfill`.

**Page 9 (静态攻击)** — ✅ Wording and layout are clean. The two-column steps/types split is well-balanced and no AI phrases detected.

**Summary of fixes needed in HUSTopening.tex:**

| Frame 5, l.167 | `本文讨论的三个具体问题` | → `待回答的三个问题` |

**P12** — 动态攻击：PPO 在防护开启的环境中训练
🔴 **图：TikZ diagram** — the edge label "状态 / 统计" at the top-right of "观测→PPO策略" arrow is clipped/truncated in the rendered PNG (the slash makes the label run outside the node boundary). Fix: use `\shortstack` or a `pos=0.5,above,font=\tiny` label with explicit line break.

🔴 **表述：AI腔** — reward formula line: `奖励 = 失效 + 隐蔽 − 被检测` — "被检测" is the passive-voice template pattern ("被…"). This is also in the .tex source at line 403 as `\bluehead{奖励}\,$=$\,失效\,$+$\,隐蔽\,$-$\,被检测`. The formula itself is fine mathematically, but the verbal rendering "被检测" as a cost term in a Chinese slide reads awkwardly. Suggest: `隐蔽性 − 检测惩罚` or keep it as a pure formula with no CJK in the minus term.

---

**P13** — 动态攻击：检测率降至 50.1%，失效率仍 100%
🔴 **表述：模板腔** — conclusion block (line 436): `3 个随机种子之间差异较大，仍待进一步验证。` — This is textbook AI hedge/template ("差异较大，仍待进一步验证" is a classic GPT filler). Replace with the actual observation, e.g. `种子间检测率波动约 ±8 pp，结论方向一致`。

---

**P14** — 动态攻击的两类典型行为模式
🔴 **图：sparkline axes misaligned** — Both TikZ plots have a Y-axis drawn `(0,0)–(0,1.18)` but the dashed threshold line is at `y=1.0` which nearly kisses the arrow tip. The axis ticks are absent, so viewers can't read scale. The Y arrow tip and threshold dashed line are visually indistinguishable from each other. Fix: add `\node[font=\tiny,black!60,left] at (0,1.0) {阈};` and push the Y-arrow to `(0,1.25)` so the dashed line has breathing room.

🔴 **图：CJK 字间距** — The block title "隐蔽—爆发切换" is fine, but in the left block, the caption `全程低幅扰动 → 误差缓慢累积` and right block `前段隐蔽 → 后段集中扰动 → 失效` are rendered as `\footnotesize` outside the TikZ and visually sit too close to the axis bottom. A `\vspace{0.3em}` before each caption would prevent crowding.

---

**P15** — 防护代价：能检测，未必能安全恢复
🔴 **表述：AI腔 × 2**

1. Line 512: `静态搜索得到的攻击参数不能直接代表防护开启后的风险` — "代表…风险" is classic 翻译腔 ("represent the risk"). Rewrite: `静态搜索得到的攻击参数，在防护开启后攻击成功率可能显著下降`。

2. Line 518: `仅增强检测覆盖，无法接管控制` — "检测覆盖" (detection coverage) and "接管控制" (take over control) are both direct English calques. Rewrite: `仅提高了触发率，车辆仍无法主动纠偏`。

🔴 **结构：列宽不平衡** — Left column is `0.56\textwidth`, right is `0.40\textwidth`. The right block ("补充：角速率 CUSUM") text wraps onto two lines (`但仅增强检测覆盖，无法接管控制` wraps as seen in PNG). With `0.40` width that's too tight. Either expand right column to `0.46` (reduce left to `0.50`), or fold the supplement text into a single shorter sentence.

---

**P16** — 总结
🔴 **表述：罗列腔** — Line 534: `检测、拒绝、停车、任务结果需要在同一闭环中分别度量` — "逐项分别…度量" matches the flagged pattern "X、Y、Z——逐项分别统计". Rewrite: `四类指标须在同一闭环中同时度量，单看检测率会高估防护效果`。

🔴 **表述：AI腔** — Line 551: `量化静态参数与防护后风险的差距` — "量化…的差距" is a direct GPT calque ("quantify the gap between…"). Rewrite: `发现静态最优幅度在防护开启后失效率显著下降`。

🔴 **结构：空旷** — The slide has a full-width "核心结论" block on top and then two half-width blocks floating in the middle with a very large empty bottom area (visible in PNG). Fix: remove one `\vfill` between the top block and the columns to pull them closer, or add a third row (e.g. a brief take-away line at the bottom).

---

**P17** — 不足与展望
🔴 **结构：右列 bullet 换行难看** — "结合 GNSS 物理层约束建模攻击" wraps mid-word between "建模" and "攻击" (visible in PNG: `约束建模攻\n击`). Rewrite bullet as `结合 GNSS 物理约束建模攻击` (drop "层") to fit on one line, or use `\item 结合物理层约束，对 GNSS 攻击建模` which breaks more naturally.

🔴 **结构：large dead space** — Both columns end at roughly 60% slide height; the bottom 40% is blank. Add `\vfill` around the columns or give each column a third bullet to balance density.

---

**P18** — 谢谢
🔴 **图：CJK 强制字间距** — "谢 谢" is rendered with an explicit space between the two characters (visible in PNG), giving an unnatural forced letter-spacing. Check the .tex source: if it's `谢\hspace{...}谢` or `谢 谢`, remove the space/hspace and use `\ziju{0.5}{谢谢}` (xeCJK) or simply `谢谢` at large font. The current look is the classic "2-char label with letter-spacing" anti-pattern mentioned in the audit criteria.

---

**Summary of priority fixes:**

| Page | Category | Line / Element | Fix |
|------|----------|----------------|-----|
| 13 | AI腔 | 436 `差异较大，仍待进一步验证` | Replace with actual ±variance number |
| 15 | AI腔 | 512 `代表防护开启后的风险` | Rewrite to direct statement |
| 15 | AI腔 | 518 `检测覆盖` / `接管控制` | Replace calques with plain Chinese |
| 16 | AI腔 | 551 `量化…的差距` | Replace with concrete finding |
| 16 | AI腔 | 534 `分别度量` list | Rewrite to a single pointed claim |
| 15 | 结构 | right col 0.40 too narrow | Widen to 0.46 |
| 16 | 结构 | empty bottom | Remove surplus `\vfill` |
| 17 | 结构 | right bullet wraps | Shorten bullet text |
| 14 | 图 | sparkline Y-axis crowded | Push arrow tip to 1.25, label threshold |
| 18 | 图 | `谢 谢` forced spacing | Remove space, use `\ziju` or plain `谢谢` |

---

## Page 1 — Title slide
✅ Mostly clean. Minor issues:
- **Dead zone**: ~35% of the slide below the date is empty white. The title block sits high-center but the lower third is bare. Consider adding `\vspace` balance or shifting the title block slightly lower.
- **Font mismatch**: `\setsansfont{Times New Roman}` forces Times on a Beamer slide; CJK via SimSun + Latin via Times New Roman renders inconsistently in the author/institute lines. The institute line "华中科技大学 人工智能与自动化学院" has a visible gap between the two halves — confirm `\;` produces the right spacing visually (it looks like two separate entities).

---

## Page 2 — 汇报提纲
🔴
- **Excessive dead space**: Four items with 1.4 em vertical gaps consume only the top 60% of the content area; the bottom 30% is blank white. Reduce `\vspace{1.4em}` in the TOC template to `0.9em`.
- **Items too large**: The `\large` font on TOC items makes this feel inflated. Drop to `\normalsize` — the content doesn't justify the size.
- **No hierarchy signal**: All four items are visually equal weight. Consider making the currently-active section item bold or using a rule to separate sections.

---

## Page 3 — 研究背景：定位攻击实验难以在真实道路上完成
🔴
- **Frametitle overlong**: The 18-character frametitle wraps or risks clipping on narrower displays. Shorten to "研究背景：定位攻击为何需要仿真" or "背景：真实道路实验的局限".
- **Two stacked equal-weight blocks** with a TikZ chain between them creates a "sandwich" that reads as three horizontal bands of equal importance. The pipeline arrow diagram floats in dead whitespace between the blocks with no label indicating what it represents. Add a `\caption`-style `{\scriptsize 实验闭环示意}` below it, or tighten vertical spacing.
- **Block body density mismatch**: Top block has 3 bullets; bottom block has 1 long sentence. The asymmetry reads as filler. Either add 1 bullet to the bottom block or convert it to plain text outside a block.

---

## Page 4 — 研究对象：定位—控制闭环
🔴
- **CJK letter-spacing visible** in the bottom-left block: "观测—估计—控制" rendered bold via `\bluehead` in the middle of a sentence causes perceptible glyph-spacing inconsistency. Verify `AutoFakeBold` isn't adding inter-character gaps.
- **TikZ label "越界/碰撞\\轨迹偏离"**: The last node is the only one with a two-line label and is wider than the others — visually asymmetric. Use "越界\\碰撞" or a single line "碰撞/越界".
- **Two side-by-side blocks of identical width/height** with no visual weight difference. "研究意义" should be primary; consider making it slightly wider (0.56/0.42 split) or using `alertblock` for 研究意义.
- **Large gap** between TikZ diagram and bottom columns (~0.8 em) wastes usable area.

---

## Page 5 — 现有研究多关注检测…
🔴
- **Frametitle 2-line risk**: "现有研究多关注检测，告警之后的车辆状态评估不足" is 24 characters — will wrap on standard aspect. Replace: "现有研究：检测已有基础，告警后评估不足".
- **Left column bullet wrap**: "GNSS 欺骗、IMU 偏置已被讨论" breaks mid-bullet to a new line "论" alone — orphan character. Fix: "GNSS 欺骗与 IMU 偏置已有讨论".
- **AI-tone phrase**: "反映实际危害" in question 2 (`还能反映\bluehead{实际危害}吗`) — not flagged-list AI-tone, acceptable. But "静态搜索得到的攻击边界" is nominalized-bureaucratic; replace: "静态搜索找到的攻击边界".
- **3-block stacked hierarchy**: Two top columns + one wide bottom block = three visual bands of identical blue-header style. The bottom block (三个问题) should be visually elevated — use `\begin{alertblock}` or increase its padding.

---

## Page 6 — 技术路线
🔴
- **TikZ snake diagram**: The two-row pipeline (1→4 top row, 5→7 bottom row right-to-left) is hard to read because row 2 reverses direction. The vertical arrow from 4→5 is the only directional cue — add a right-angle bend label or use a U-turn arrow style.
- **说明 block text wrap**: "先搭仿真闭环和静态攻击边界，加入防护做对比，再用 PPO 研究\bluehead{动态攻击}。" wraps after "攻" leaving "击。" as a 2-char orphan on line 2. Rebreak: "先搭仿真闭环与静态攻击边界；加入防护对比；再用 PPO 研究\bluehead{动态攻击者}。"
- **Footnote italic**: The gray italic disclaimer `PSO 结果是当前参数设置下的候选最优` uses `\footnotesize\itshape\color{black!60}` inside a block — visually inconsistent (block body is upright). Move to a `\vspace{0.3em}\par{\footnotesize...}` outside the block, or keep but remove `\itshape`.
- **Dead zone**: ~25% of slide below the block is empty.

---

## TOP 3 PRIORITY FIXES

**1. Frametitle overflow (Pages 3, 5) — highest visual impact.**
Both are 20+ character titles that will wrap or crowd the frametitle bar. Shorten Page 3 to "背景：定位攻击实验为何需要仿真" and Page 5 to "现有研究：检测已有基础，告警后评估不足". Single-line frametitles are mandatory for the Madrid bar.

**2. 3-stacked equal-weight blocks (Pages 3, 5) — hierarchy is invisible.**
On both slides, the bottom wide block (做法 / 待回答的三个问题) deserves higher visual weight than the upper pair. Change those two to `\begin{alertblock}{...}` — this uses the Madrid alert color (still blue in whale theme) and instantly signals primary content without adding orange.

**3. TOC dead space + oversized items (Page 2) — wastes a full slide.**
Reduce `\vspace{1.4em}` to `0.7em` in the TOC section template and change `\large` to `\normalsize`. This removes the bottom-third blank zone and makes the TOC look intentionally designed rather than auto-stretched.

## Page 13 — 动态攻击：检测率降至 50.1%，失效率仍 100%

🔴

- **Grey row readability**: "屏蔽诊断维度（补充实验）" row text is light grey on white — contrast ratio fails WCAG AA. Fix: change row color from `\rowcolor{gray!15}` to `\rowcolor{blue!8}` or simply drop the row color and use `\color{gray!60}` only on the text, keeping the row background white.
- **AI-tone wording**: "消融性验证" in the 备注 column — no Chinese speaker naturally writes this in spoken defense context. Replace with "屏蔽维度后的对照实验" or simply "对照验证".
- **Vertical rhythm**: large dead white band between frametitle and table (~30% of slide height). The table + conclusion block only occupies the bottom half. Move content upward with `\vspace{-0.5em}` before the table or remove the explicit `\vspace` spacer.

---

## Page 14 — 动态攻击的两类典型行为模式

✅ (mostly)

- **Threshold label**: The dashed "失效" horizontal label on both charts sits flush against the y-axis label — the two strings visually collide at small rendering size. Add `\hspace{2pt}` or shift the label anchor to `right` alignment.
- **Chart symmetry**: Left chart x-extent appears slightly wider than right chart. If both use the same `xmax`, ensure identical `width=` in pgfplots to maintain mirror symmetry.
- **Bottom block**: Wording "由此规避检测，又能让横向误差最终越界" is slightly AI-clause-stacked. Natural alternative: "每步扰动低于阈值，但误差持续积累直至失效".

---

## Page 15 — 防护代价：能检测，未必能安全恢复

🔴

- **Left block line wrap**: "防护检测到攻击，并不等于车辆已经回到安全状态。" wraps mid-sentence creating a widow "状态。" line plus the continuation "静态搜索拿到的攻击幅度，在防护开启后未必仍能造成失效。" — two full sentences crammed into a narrow block. Shorten to: "检测到攻击 ≠ 车辆恢复安全；静态最优幅度在防护开启后未必有效。"
- **X symbol color**: The red `×` for IMU row is visually prominent but "未抑制" in the same row is also red bold — double-red creates redundancy. Drop bold from "未抑制" or use `\color{darkblue}` for the text.
- **Right block font size**: "77 次预设 IMU 姿态攻击均能**5 s 内**触发" — the bold "5 s" mid-sentence breaks rhythm. Use `\textbf{5\,s}` for correct thin space.

---

## Page 16 — 总结

🔴

- **Dead vertical space**: ~35% empty white above the "核心结论" block. The three blocks are all pushed to the bottom two-thirds. Add `\vspace*{-1.2em}` after `\begin{frame}` or use `[t]` frame option to anchor content high.
- **Two-column height mismatch**: Right block ("实验层面") has a bullet that wraps to second line ("著失效") making it taller than the left block — the bottoms are misaligned. Either equalize with `minipage` of fixed height or shorten: "发现静态最优幅度在防护下显著失效" → single line.
- **Frametitle**: "总结" is two characters — feels sparse for a full dark frametitle bar. Consider "总结与贡献" for visual balance.

---

## Page 17 — 不足与展望

🔴

- **Dead top half**: Content (two side-by-side blocks) sits entirely in the bottom 40% of the slide. The top 60% is pure white. This is the worst vertical centering issue in the set. Fix: add `\vfill` before the columns or use `[c]` frame alignment with `\vspace{0pt plus 1fill}` above.
- **"暂未"**: sounds apologetic/AI-hedged. Replace with "当前未引入" for crisper academic tone.

---

## Page 18 — 谢谢

✅ (mostly)

- **Letter spacing on 谢谢**: The manual kerning between the two characters is very wide (~1 em gap). This is a known CJK rendering artifact with `\CJKsetecglue`. The gap looks decorative but slightly off — tighten to `\ziju{0.5}` or `0.3em` inter-character space.
- **Info line**: "王致尧 · 华中科技大学人工智能与自动化学院 · 2026 年 5 月" — the `·` separator is not vertically centered with CJK glyphs. Use `\textbullet` or `\enspace·\enspace` for consistent vertical alignment.
- **Color**: 谢谢 uses dark blue matching theme — good. No orange present — compliant.

---

## TOP 3 PRIORITY FIXES

**1. P17 vertical centering (highest impact)**: The two-column block floats in the bottom 40% of a blank slide. Add `\vfill\begin{columns}...\end{columns}\vfill` or use `[c]` frame option. Looks unfinished as-is.

**2. P16 dead space + block height mismatch**: Same dead-top issue, plus the misaligned column bottoms undermine the summary slide. Wrap columns in fixed-height minipages and pull content upward.

**3. P13 grey row contrast + "消融性验证" wording**: The grey supplementary row fails contrast and the term "消融性验证" is an AI-ism that will read poorly to committee members. Change row shading to `blue!8` and replace with "对照验证".

## Page 7 — 实验平台：CARLA 闭环 + 攻击中间件 + 运行时防护

🔴

**TikZ feedback loop overshoots left boundary.** The dashed return path `(vehicle.west) -| (sensor.south)` creates a vertical drop below the node row and a long horizontal run leftward. The left-side turn of this loop is invisible or exits the visible area; the leftward extension of the dashed line appears to clip near the edge. Fix: use `bend left` or an explicit intermediate coordinate anchored inside the slide margins, e.g. `\draw[loop] (ctrl.south) -- ++(0,-0.7) -| (sensor.south);` — route the return below all five boxes at a controlled y-offset.

**Excessive vertical whitespace above diagram.** The `\vfill` before the TikZ and the large gap above the pipeline boxes leave ~30% of the slide empty. Reduce top `\vfill` to `\vspace{0.4em}`.

**Node label "路径跟踪"** is narrower than its box; `minimum width=1.95cm` fits but looks loose. Minor; acceptable.

---

## Page 8 — 场景库与评价指标

🔴

**Table floats in a sea of whitespace.** The table is centre-aligned with no surrounding text, leaving large blank bands above and below. Tighten with `\vspace{0.3em}` instead of `\vfill` above the table.

**Right block text overflow.** "检测触发、测量拒绝、停车响应、任务完成四项在同一回合中独立记录。" runs to two dense lines that almost overflow the block. Split into a compact itemize of 4 items.

**AI-tone wording** — "同一回合中独立记录" reads neutrally, no issue. No AI-tone problems on this page.

## Page 9 — 静态攻击：先网格扫描，再 PSO 精细搜索

✅

No structural defects. Numbered list maps cleanly to attack types column. Spacing and block proportions are well-balanced. One minor point: the top `\vfill` + block gap leaves roughly 0.5 cm dead space above the 目的 block — acceptable but could use `\vspace{0.2em}` for tighter rhythm.

---

## Page 10 — 静态攻击：道路几何决定有效阈值

🔴

**Footnote italic font is too small and low-contrast.** `\footnotesize\itshape\color{black!60}` in a slide context renders at ~6 pt equivalent — illegible from 3 m. Change to `\small\color{black!70}` (drop italic).

**Bold semantics ambiguous.** The same bold face marks both the minimum (弯道) and maximum (路口) values per row — the footnote explains this but is unreadable. Consider using a blue color (`\textcolor{structure.fg}{\textbf{...}}`) for minima and red for maxima, or add a visible legend.

**结论 block text** "弯道阈值最小，路口最大；三类攻击的排序一致，漂移攻击在不同定位变体之间复现最稳定。" — the phrase "复现最稳定" sounds slightly AI-generated. Replace with "漂移攻击在三类变体间阈值差异最小" (concrete and data-grounded).

---

## Page 11 — 防护回放：GNSS 类被抑制，IMU 仍可造成失效

🔴

**Red color in table cell violates the no-orange, theme-neutral palette rule** but introduces `red!75!black` which is not in the Madrid/whale theme palette. This is visually jarring. Replace with `\textcolor{structure.fg}{\textbf{...}}` for consistency — the dark-blue bold is already prominent enough; the 结论 block provides the verbal callout.

**"综合" column is a freeform text column** (有效抑制/部分抑制/未抑制) flush-left while numeric columns are centered. Source uses `@{}lcccl@{}` — the `l` for the last column is correct but creates visual misalignment because it is the only left-aligned column among centered ones. Move "综合" column to a footnote or replace with a color-coded symbol to remove the mixed-alignment issue.

**Footnote** same low-contrast italic issue as P10.

---

## Page 12 — 动态攻击：PPO 在防护开启的环境中训练

🔴

**Edge label "状态 / 统计" is clipped.** In the PNG, the label above the obs→policy arrow is partially cropped by the node boundary — the right portion of "统计" sits nearly on top of the PPO 策略 node border. Fix: add `xshift=-6pt` to the midpoint node, or use `[above,midway,xshift=-0.3cm]` anchor.

**Loop diagram is a rectangle of four boxes** — the "观测" box (left) and "奖励信号" box (below-left) are the same width as "PPO 策略" and "CARLA 闭环," but "观测" carries only two characters. The `minimum width=3.0cm` forces unnecessary padding. Reduce 观测 and 奖励信号 to `minimum width=2.2cm`.

**左列 block text** "观测 = 车辆状态 / 防护统计 / 攻击历史 / 奖励 = 失效 + 隐蔽性 − 检测惩罚" uses math-mode spacing (`\,$=$\,`) inside CJK text — the spacing is inconsistent with surrounding characters. Replace with a two-item `\itemize` for visual clarity.

---

## TOP 3 PRIORITY FIXES

**1. P7 TikZ feedback loop routing (highest visual impact)** — the dashed return arrow currently overshoots or clips the left margin. Reroute as explicit coordinates: `\draw[loop] (ctrl.south) -- ++(0,-0.55) -| ([xshift=-4pt]sensor.south);` This is the most visible structural defect across all six pages.

**2. P10/P11 footnote legibility** — `\footnotesize\itshape\color{black!60}` is unreadable in a projected environment. Change both footnotes to `\small\color{black!70}` (no italic). Affects two slides and is immediately noticeable.

**3. P11 red color in table** — `\textcolor{red!75!black}` breaks the Madrid/whale theme palette and produces a visually harsh accent. Replace with `\textcolor{structure.fg}{\textbf{25.0\%}}` and `\textcolor{structure.fg}{\textbf{未抑制}}` to keep the callout within the theme's dark-blue vocabulary.

120	    \draw[arr] (obs)--(atk); \draw[arr] (atk)--(ekf);
121	    \draw[arr] (ekf)--(ctrl); \draw[arr] (ctrl)--(res);
122	    \node[lab,below=5pt of obs]  {传感链};
123	    \node[lab,below=5pt of atk]  {威胁面};
124	    \node[lab,below=5pt of ekf]  {状态估计};
125	    \node[lab,below=5pt of ctrl] {决策与执行};
126	    \node[lab,below=5pt of res]  {闭环失效};
127	  \end{tikzpicture}
128	  \vspace{0.8em}
129	  \begin{columns}[T,onlytextwidth]
130	    \begin{column}{0.485\textwidth}
131	      \begin{block}{研究意义}
132	        在仿真中观察定位攻击如何沿\bluehead{观测—估计—控制}传播到车辆轨迹。
133	      \end{block}
134	    \end{column}
135	    \hspace{0.02\textwidth}
136	    \begin{column}{0.485\textwidth}
137	      \begin{block}{研究边界}
138	        只研究\bluehead{定位链路}的攻击与防护，不涉及感知、决策与其他信息安全问题。
139	      \end{block}
140	    \end{column}
141	  \end{columns}
142	\end{frame}
143	
144	% ===== 5 现有问题与三个研究问题 =====
145	\begin{frame}
146	  \frametitle{现有研究偏检测，告警之后的车辆状态评估不足}
147	  \vfill
148	  \begin{columns}[T,onlytextwidth]
149	    \begin{column}{0.485\textwidth}
150	      \begin{block}{已有基础}
151	        \begin{itemize}\setlength{\itemsep}{0.18em}
152	          \item CARLA 支撑闭环驾驶仿真已成熟
153	          \item GNSS 欺骗与 IMU 偏置已有讨论
154	        \end{itemize}
155	      \end{block}
156	    \end{column}
157	    \hspace{0.02\textwidth}
158	    \begin{column}{0.485\textwidth}
159	      \begin{block}{尚未充分回答}
160	        \begin{itemize}\setlength{\itemsep}{0.18em}
161	          \item 不同场景下的最小有效幅度
162	          \item 防护告警之后的\bluehead{车辆实际状态}
163	        \end{itemize}
164	      \end{block}
165	    \end{column}
166	  \end{columns}
167	  \vfill
168	  \begin{block}{待回答的三个问题}
169	    \begin{enumerate}\setlength{\itemsep}{0.18em}
170	      \item 不同道路几何下，让车辆失效所需的\bluehead{最小攻击幅度}相差多少？
171	      \item 加入防护后，静态搜索找到的攻击边界还能反映\bluehead{实际危害}吗？
172	      \item 经过学习的\bluehead{动态攻击者}是否会改变上述评估？
173	    \end{enumerate}
174	  \end{block}
175	  \vfill
176	\end{frame}
